                                                                        Italy's Eni and France's TotalEnergies have completed
                                                                    the divestments of a string of Nigerian onshore oil assets,
                                                                    transferring them to indigenous producers, as international oil
                                                                    companies focus on deepwater projects in the African country.

                                                                        In a statement released late Oct. 26, Eni said it had concluded
                                                                    the sale of its Nigerian subsidiary, Agip Energy and Natural
                                                                    Resources, or AENR, to Apex Global Energy, a company wholly
                                                                    owned by Nigerian firm Sterling Oil Exploration and Energy
                                                                    Production.

                                                                        The transaction comes two years after Eni sold four Nigerian
                                                                    onshore producing oil blocks - OMLs 60, 61, 62 and 63 - as well as
                                                                    stakes in the Brass terminal, exploration concessions and power
                                                                    plants, to another Nigerian upstream operator, Oando.

                                                                        The latest divestment sees Eni selling off AENR's interests
                                                                    in onshore and shallow-water oil and gas exploration and
                                                                    production assets in Nigeria, including a 5% stake in the
                                                                    Renaissance joint venture, where it was partnered with
                                                                    TotalEnergies and state-run Nigerian National Petroleum Co.

                                                                        "The transaction, which has received approval from all
                                                                    relevant authorities, is in line with Eni's strategy to optimize its
                                                                    upstream activities by rebalancing the portfolio and divesting

Malaysia's Sarawak adds 800 b/d
of onshore oil output: official

Sarawak producing half of Malaysia's oil

65-km Samalaju pipeline targets 2026 finish

Kuching LNG terminal aims for 2029 startup

    Malaysia's Sarawak has brought about 800 barrels/day of
onshore oil production online and is advancing gas infrastructure
to supply growing industrial and power demand, Deputy Minister
of Energy and Environmental Sustainability of Sarawak Dr. Haji
Hazland bin Abang Hipni said at the Singapore International
Energy Week Oct. 27.

To:  Symbol   Bates Dec Freq Curr  UOM  Description                                                        · Type: Volume Unit
MDC  PFEEB00  lhc 2 QR EUR         CPA  Platts FOLicht Ethanol Beverage 96 Grade                           · Definition: A unit of volume that measures the volume of pure
BFF  PFEEA03  lhc 3 MA EUR         CPA  DAP Central Europe Eur/CPA Contract Qtly                           (100%) alcohol. One HLTPA equals 1 hectoliter (100 liters) of pure
BFF  PFEEA04  lhc 2 WA EUR         CPA  Platts FOLicht Ethanol Beverage 96 Grade                           alcohol. Unlike a standard hectoliter (hL), which measures the
BFF  PFEED00  lhc 2 QR EUR         CPA  DAP Central Europe Eur/CPA MAvg                                    total volume of any liquid, HLTPA refers only to the pure alcohol
BFF  PFEEC03  lhc 3 MA EUR         CPA  Platts FOLicht Ethanol Beverage 96 Grade                           content, regardless of the concentration in the original beverage.
